From the mid-2000s, emergence of [tube sites](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_online_video_platforms) led to an increase in free streaming and a decrease in traditional studio sales.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEHayes2014-164][^cite_note-FOOTNOTEKelsey2012-165] Many performers turned to [subscription-based](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Subscription_business_model) platforms like [OnlyFans](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/OnlyFans), which continue to provide financial independence for some, but also increase market saturation, making it harder for new creators to establish themselves. Additionally, dependence on third-party platforms leaves creators vulnerable to policy changes and financial restrictions.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEHolmes2021-166] In 2020, [Visa](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Visa_Inc.) and [Mastercard](378791.md) implemented restrictions on processing payments for adult content due to concerns over illegal material.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTECole2020c-167] Additionally, the arrival of [AI-generated](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/AI-generated) adult content, including [deepfake](56641018.md) pornography, poses ethical and legal dilemmas.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEDubéLapointe2024-168]

### Economics

The production and [distribution](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Distribution_\(business\)) of pornography are economic activities of some importance. In Europe, [Budapest](36787.md) is regarded as the industry center.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEBridge1996-169][^cite_note-FOOTNOTEZook2005-170] Other pornography production centers in the world are located in Florida (US), Brazil, Czech Republic, and Japan.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEGoldsteinSteinbergAynalemKerndt2011-107] In the United States, the pornography industry employs about 20,000 people including 2,000 to 3,000 performers,[^cite_note-FOOTNOTETarrant201649-171] and is centered in the [San Fernando Valley](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/San_Fernando_Valley) of Los Angeles. By 2005, it became the largest pornography production center in the world.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTERodriguez-HartChitaleRiggGoldstein2012-106][^cite_note-FOOTNOTEGoldsteinSteinbergAynalemKerndt2011-107] Apart from regular media coverage, the industry in the US receives considerable attention from private organizations, government agencies, and political organizations.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEAVN15_July_1999-172]



A [vending machine](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Vending_machine) in Japan selling adult entertainment products. In 2018, [pornography in Japan](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pornography_in_Japan) was estimated to be worth over \$20 billion.

[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEStrusiewicz2018-173]

[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEHambleton2019361-174]

As of 2011, pornography was becoming one of the biggest businesses in the United States.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTED'Orlando2011-175] In 2014, the porn industry was believed to bring in at least \$13 billion on a yearly basis in the United States.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTESzymanskiStewart-Richardson2014-176] Through the 2010s, many pornography production companies and top pornographic websites such as [Pornhub](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pornhub), [RedTube](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/RedTube) and [YouPorn](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/YouPorn) have been acquired by [MindGeek](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/MindGeek), a company that has been described as "a monopoly" in the pornography business.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEAuerbach2014-177] This development was identified as a problem. According to Marina Adshade, a professor from the [Vancouver School of Economics](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Vancouver_School_of_Economics) and the author of *Dollars and Sex: How economics influences sex and love*, having a monopoly in the pornography business has forced the producers to reduce their charges, and radically changed the work of performers "who are now under greater pressure to perform acts that they would have been able to refuse in the past", all at a lower price without profits for themselves.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEHarford2019-77] Some pornographic productions are often linked to [prostitution](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Prostitution).[^cite_note-178][^cite_note-179][^cite_note-180]

Online pornography is available both for a fee and free of charge.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEDöring2009-115] The availability of free porn on the Internet has led to a decline in the business of mainstream pornography.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEKopp202011-181][^cite_note-FOOTNOTEHarford2019-77] Piracy is estimated to result in losses of some \$2 billion a year for the porn industry.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEMacmillen2017-129] Budgets of many studios reduced considerably and contracts for performers became less common.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEMacmillen2017-129] Reportedly, applications by established [pornography companies](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_pornography_companies) for porn-shoot permits in [Los Angeles County](79734.md) fell by 95 percent during the period 2012 to 2015.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEKopp202011-181][^cite_note-FOOTNOTEForrester2016-102] According to Mark Spiegler, an adult talent agent, in the early 2000s female performers made about \$100,000 a year. By 2017, the amount is about \$50,000.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEMacmillen2017-129]

The technological era led to decline of the studio and "the rise of the pornography worker herself".[^cite_note-FOOTNOTELubey2022215-182] Newer ways of monetization have opened for the pornography workers who are taking the path of entrepreneurship.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTELubey2022215-182] In 1995, [Jenna Jameson](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jenna_Jameson) signed her first contract with the porn studio [Wicked Pictures](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wicked_Pictures).[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEForrester2016-102] After building a brand image for herself she started her own company [ClubJenna](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/ClubJenna), which by 2005 was reportedly earning an annual revenue of \$30-\$35 million.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEForrester2016-102][^cite_note-FOOTNOTEMacmillen2017-129] "Performers are hustlers now," said [Chanel Preston](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Chanel_Preston) (a performer who was also chairperson of the [Adult Performer Advocacy Committee](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Adult_Performer_Advocacy_Committee)), while noting that performers have to be creative to sustain their income and reach audience, both of which, she said are mainly achieved through "feature dancing, selling merchandise, webcamming", among other activities.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEMacmillen2017-129] "Custom" pornography made according to the requests of customer clients has emerged as one new business niche.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEHarford2019-77] The average career for the new age performer lasts about four to six months.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEForrester2016-102] Before moving on to the business side, adult performers use studio works to advertise and build a brand image for themselves. They acquire an audience who would later pay at personal website or [webcam performances](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Webcam_model).[^cite_note-FOOTNOTELubey2022215-182][^cite_note-FOOTNOTEMacmillen2017-129] Commercial webcamming, which emerged in the 1990s[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEWeitzer2023195-183] as a niche sector in the adult entertainment industry, grew to become a multibillion-dollar business by the mid-2020s.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEWeitzer202314-184]

The exact economic size of the porn industry in the early-twenty-first century is unknown to anyone.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTERosen2023-121] Kassia Wosick, a sociologist from New Mexico State University, estimated the global porn market value at \$97 billion in 2015, with the US revenue estimated at \$10 and \$12 billion. IBISWorld, a leading researcher of various markets and industries, calculated total US revenue to reach \$3.3 billion by 2020.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTERosen2023-121][^cite_note-FOOTNOTENBCnews20_January_2015-185] On the basis of a research report by a market analysis firm, *[USA Today](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/USA_Today)* published that the estimated worth of the adult entertainment industry market in 2023 is over \$172 billion.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEAustin2024-186]

### Technology

Pornographers have taken advantage of each major technological advancement for the production and distribution of their services.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEBarss20103-187] Pornography has been called an "erotic engine" and a driving force in the development of various media related technologies from the [printing press](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Printing_press), through photography (still and motion), to [satellite TV](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Satellite_TV), [Home video](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Home_video), and [streaming media](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Streaming_media).[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEBarss20101,10-188]

One of the world's leading anti-pornography campaigners, [Gail Dines](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gail_Dines), has stated that "the demand for porn has driven the development of core cross-platform technologies for [data compression](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Data_compression), [search](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Search_engine), [transmission](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Data_communication) and [micro-payments](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Micro-payments)."[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEMcNair201323-32] Many of the technological developments that had been led by pornography have benefited other fields of human activity too.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEMcNair201323-32] In the early 2000s, [Wicked Pictures](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wicked_Pictures) pushed for the adoption of the [MPEG-4](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/MPEG-4) file format ahead of others, this later became the most commonly used format across high-speed Internet connections.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTENowak2011210-189] In 2009, [Pink Visual](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pink_Visual) became one of the first companies to license and produce content with a software introduced by a small Toronto-based company called "Spatial view", which later made it possible to view [3D content](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/3D_computer_graphics) on [iPhones](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/IPhone).[^cite_note-FOOTNOTENowak201114-190]

As an early adopter of innovations, the pornography industry has been cited to be a crucial factor in the development and popularization of various media processing and communication technologies.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTENowak201114-190][^cite_note-FOOTNOTEMcNair201310-191] From innovative smaller film cameras, to the [VCRs](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/VCR), and the Internet, the porn industry has employed newer technologies much ahead than other commercial industries, this early adoption provided the developers their early [financial capital](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Financial_capital), which aided in the further development of these technologies.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTENowak20114-192][^cite_note-FOOTNOTEBarss20102-193] The success of innovative technologies is predicted by their greater use in the porn industry.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEAVN11_March_2014-194]

> The way you know if your technology is good and solid is if it's doing well in the porn world.

— Susan Struble, spokesperson of [Sun Microsystems](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sun_Microsystems)

[^cite_note-FOOTNOTENowak2011198-195]

Pornographic content accounted for most [videotape](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Videotape) sales during the late 1970s.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEHarford2019-77] The pornography industry has been considered an influential factor in deciding the [format wars](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Format_war) in media, including being a factor in the [VHS](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/VHS) vs. [Betamax](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Betamax) format war (the [videotape format war](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Videotape_format_war))[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEMearian2006-196][^cite_note-FOOTNOTELynch2007-197] and the [Blu-ray](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Blu-ray) vs. [HD DVD](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/HD_DVD) format war (the [high-def format war](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/High_definition_optical_disc_format_war)).[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEMearian2006-196][^cite_note-FOOTNOTELynch2007-197][^cite_note-FOOTNOTEGardiner2007-198] Piracy, the illegal copying and distribution of material, is of great concern to the porn industry.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEHymes2009-199] The industry has been the subject of many litigations and formalized anti-piracy efforts.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEKernes2014a-200][^cite_note-FOOTNOTEAVN13_May_2014-201]

Many of the innovative data rendering procedures, enhanced payment systems, customer service models, and security methods developed by pornography companies have been co-opted by other mainstream businesses.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTENowak2011210–211-202][^cite_note-FOOTNOTEBarss20101-203] Pornography companies served as the basis for a large number of innovations in [web development](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Web_development). Much of the [IT work](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/IT) in porn companies is done by people who are referred to as a "porn webmaster", often paid well in what are small businesses, they have more freedom to test innovations compared to other IT employees in larger organizations who tend to be risk-averse.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTENowak2011212-204]

#### Virtual reality pornography



Attendees viewing virtual reality pornography at the [Venus Berlin](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Venus_Berlin) adult entertainment convention, 2018



Virtual reality pornography shooting in [Venus Berlin](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Venus_Berlin) 2019

**Virtual reality pornography**, commonly known as **VR pornography**, refers to pornography designed to be experienced through [virtual reality](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Virtual_reality) technology. It typically involves immersive, interactive videos or simulations that users view using [virtual reality headsets](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Virtual_reality_headset). These experiences often provide a 360-degree perspective, allowing the user to feel as though they are part of the scene, enhancing the sense of presence and realism compared to traditional pornography. The content can range from pre-recorded videos to more interactive, computer-generated environments, sometimes incorporating haptic feedback devices for a multi-sensory experience.

The idea of [computer-generated](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Computer-generated_imagery) pornography was conceived very early as one of the obvious areas of application for computer graphics. Until the late 1990s, digitally manipulated pornography could not be produced cost-effectively. In the early 2000s, it became a growing segment as the modeling and animation software matured, and the rendering capabilities of computers improved. Further advances in technology allowed increasingly photorealistic 3D figures to be used in interactive pornography.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEColeMaiberg2019b-205][^cite_note-FOOTNOTEKrueger2017-206][^cite_note-FOOTNOTEGriffin2017-207] The first pornographic film to be shot in 3D was *[3D Sex and Zen: Extreme Ecstasy](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/3D_Sex_and_Zen:_Extreme_Ecstasy)*, released on 14 April 2011, in Hong Kong.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEThe_Independent10_August_2010-208]

The various mediums for pornography depictions have evolved throughout the course of history, starting from prehistoric [cave paintings](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cave_paintings), about forty millennia ago, to futuristic virtual reality renditions.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEWeiss2020-209][^cite_note-FOOTNOTEHay2021-210][^cite_note-FOOTNOTEBarss20101-203] Experts in the pornography business predict more people in the future would consume porn through [virtual reality headsets](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Virtual_reality_headset), which are expected to give consumers better personal experiences than they can have in the real world.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEGriffin2017-207] Speculations are rife about an increased presence of [sex robots](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sex_robots) in the future pornography productions.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEHarford2019-77]

The first VR porn experiences were filmed by [VR Bangers](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/VR_Bangers?action=edit\&redlink=1). During the [COVID-19 pandemic](62750956.md), virtual reality pornography saw a noticeable increase in users.

### VR.Stripchat.com

vr.stripchat.com is a virtual reality adult streaming platform operated by [Stripchat](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Stripchat). It allows users to join live, interactive cam shows in full 3D environments directly from a browser. The platform supports major headsets including [Meta Quest](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Meta_Quest), Pico, [Apple Vision Pro](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Apple_Vision_Pro), and [Samsung Galaxy XR](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Samsung_Galaxy_XR), with real-time interactivity, [teledildonics](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Teledildonics), and a feature to switch between traditional 2D and immersive VR modes.

### BaDoinkVR

[BaDoinkVR](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/BaDoinkVR) is a [virtual reality](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Virtual_reality) [porn](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porn) production company founded in 2006,[^cite_note-211] headquartered in [Rochester, New York](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rochester,_New_York), with additional offices in [Barcelona, Spain](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Barcelona,_Spain), and [Silicon Valley](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Silicon_Valley).[^cite_note-212] Originally an online adult entertainment site,[^cite_note-213] it transitioned into virtual reality in 2015 with the launch of BaDoinkVR.[^cite_note-214] The company produces immersive 360 and 180-degree videos featuring [motion tracking](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Motion_capture) and [binaural audio](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Binaural_hearing), compatible with VR headsets such as [Oculus Rift](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Oculus_Rift), [HTC Vive](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/HTC_Vive), and [Google Cardboard](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Google_Cardboard).[^cite_note-215] Recognized as an early pioneer in the VR porn market,[^cite_note-216] BaDoinkVR won the 2018 [AVN Award](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/AVN_Award) for VR Site of the Year.[^cite_note-217] It has partnered with [Kiiroo](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/KIIROO?action=edit\&redlink=1) to integrate [teledildonics](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Teledildonics), enabling users to synchronize sex toys with VR content.[^cite_note-218] In 2015, BaDoinkVR responded to a malware incident involving the misuse of its brand to distribute [Reveton/IcePol ransomware](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ransomware#Reveton), addressing the issue and notifying authorities.[^cite_note-219]

### SexLikeReal

[SexLikeReal](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/SexLikeReal) is a [virtual reality](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Virtual_reality) pornography platform launched in 2015 by Alex Novak,[^cite_note-220] functioning as a content-sharing site, [VR](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Virtual_reality) live cam streaming service, production company, and technology developer. It is one of the largest platforms for VR porn, aggregating content from major studios like VR Bangers and [BaDoinkVR](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/BaDoinkVR) alongside its own productions.[^cite_note-221] SLR developed a VR app compatible with headsets such as [Oculus Quest](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Oculus_Quest), [Valve Index](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Valve_Index), and [HTC Vive](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/HTC_Vive), supporting high-resolution video playback and interactive [teledildonics](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Teledildonics).[^cite_note-222] The platform reported over 1.5 million monthly users in 2020[^cite_note-223] and is noted for innovations like the release of 8K VR porn videos in 2022[^cite_note-224] and advancements in [passthrough technology](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Augmented_reality) for augmented reality experiences.[^cite_note-225]

### VRPorn.com

[VRPorn.com](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/VRPorn.com) is an American [virtual reality pornography](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Virtual_reality_sex) website established in 2013[^cite_note-226] and based in [Los Angeles](18110.md).[^cite_note-227] Initially a VR fan site, it pivoted to adult content in 2016.[^cite_note-228] The site provides free access with an optional premium membership, supporting headsets like [Oculus Quest](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Oculus_Quest), [PlayStation VR](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/PlayStation_VR), and [Google Cardboard](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Google_Cardboard), as well as [WebXR](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/WebXR) streaming. By 2017, VRPorn.com was the highest-traffic VR website, recording over 8 million monthly visits.[^cite_note-229]

### VirtualRealPorn

[VirtualRealPorn](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/VirtualRealPorn) is a Spanish company founded in 2013 that produces [virtual reality](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Virtual_reality) pornographic films.[^cite_note-auto-230] Headquartered in [Zaragoza](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Zaragoza), with offices in [Granada](38264.md) and [Barcelona](4443.md), it is credited as one of the first companies to create VR porn.[^cite_note-auto-230] Its videos are compatible with VR headsets and mobile devices. In 2015, VirtualRealPorn collaborated with Kiiroo and [Lovense](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lovense) to synchronize sex toys with its VR content.[^cite_note-231] Since 2017, it has contributed to the development of *VRLove*, a multiplayer VR game featuring pornographic content.[^cite_note-232] The company was named the best VR porn site by *[The Daily Dot](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Daily_Dot)* in 2017.[^cite_note-233]

## Consumption

Pornography is a product made by adults for the consumption by adults,[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEKlein2016-234] the consumption of which has become more common among people due to the expansive use of the [Internet](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Internet).[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEZattoniGülSoligoMorlacco2021-235] About 90% of pornography is consumed on the Internet with consumers preferring content that is in tune with their [sexuality](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sexuality).[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEUdeM2009-236][^cite_note-FOOTNOTEAnthony2012-237] Pornography has been found to be a significant influencer of people's ideas about sex in the [digital age](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Digital_age).[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEAshtonMcDonaldKirkman2020-125] Pornographic websites rank among the [top 50 most-visited websites](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_most-visited_websites) worldwide.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEBőtheTóth-KirályZsilaGriffiths2018-238] [XVideos](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/XVideos) and [Pornhub](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pornhub) are the two most-visited pornographic websites worldwide.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEFritzMalicFuPaul2022-239]

Pornography consumption in people is found to induce "psychological moods and emotions" similar to those evoked during actual [sexual intercourse](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sexual_intercourse) and [casual sex](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Casual_sex).[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEBurtăverdeJonasonGiosanEne2021-240] Researchers identified four broad motivating factors for pornography consumption: an innate sexual drive or desire, to learn about sex and improve ones own sexual performance, peer pressure or social groups, lack of sexual relationship or absence of partner.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEBurtăverdeJonasonGiosanEne2021-240] The majority of pornography consumers tend to be male, unmarried, with higher levels of education.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEDöring2009-115] Younger people are more frequent consumers of porn than older people. There has been a gradual increase in the consumption rates across different age groups with the increased availability of free porn over the Internet.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEWeitzer20237-241]

Researchers at [McGill University](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/McGill_University) ascertained that on viewing pornographic content, men reached their maximum [arousal](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sexual_arousal) in about 11 minutes and women in about 12 minutes.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEBlue2009-242] An average visit to a pornographic website lasts for 11.6 minutes.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEEdelman2009212-243] Both marriage and divorce are found to be associated with lower subscription rates for adult entertainment websites.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEEdelman2009217-244] Subscriptions are more widespread in regions that have higher measures of [social capital](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Social_capital).[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEEdelman2009218-245] Pornographic websites are most often visited during office hours.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEMontopoli2010-246] As per a recent CNBC report, seventy per cent of online-porn access in the US happens between nine-to-five hours.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEForrester2016-102]

Sexual arousal and sexual enhancement tend to be the primary motivations among the self-reported reasons by users for their pornography consumption.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEZattoniGülSoligoMorlacco2021-235] Studies had found that greater levels of psychological distress leads to higher rates of pornography consumption.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEZattoniGülSoligoMorlacco2021-235] Pornography may provide a temporary relief from [stress](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Psychological_stress), or [anxiety](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Anxiety). A need to assuage [coping](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Coping) and [boredom](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Boredom) is also found to result in higher consumption of pornography.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEZattoniGülSoligoMorlacco2021-235]



Pornography use is considered a widespread recreational activity among people in-line with other digitally mediated activities such as use of [social media](5897742.md) or [video games](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Video_games).

[^cite_note-use-3]

### By gender

See also: [Straight women's preference for lesbian pornography](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Straight_women's_preference_for_lesbian_pornography)

Annually since 2013, [Pornhub](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pornhub) Insights has released a "Year In Review" report (except in 2020 due to the [COVID-19 pandemic](62750956.md)).[^cite_note-248] The data found that the [lesbian category](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lesbian_pornography) has been consistently the most popular among female viewers since 2014 when gender statistics were first gathered, and that women in general regardless of [sexual orientation](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sexual_orientation) are more likely to search for lesbian-associated terms such as "[scissoring](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Scissoring)" than men, who are typically the target audience of such content.[^cite_note-249] Several articles; including those by [Cosmopolitan](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cosmopolitan_\(magazine\)), [Glamour](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Glamour_\(magazine\)), and [Women's Health](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Women's_Health_\(magazine\)) magazines; have supported these findings through research of their own.[^cite_note-250][^cite_note-251][^cite_note-252] Furthermore, [gay male pornography](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gay_pornography) ranked as the second most preferred category for female visitors before its statistics were separated into its own section starting in 2016.[^cite_note-253][^cite_note-254][^cite_note-255] According to data scientist [Seth Stephens-Davidowitz](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Seth_Stephens-Davidowitz), 25% of female searches for heterosexual content on the site involved keywords for [painful](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Algolagnia), [humiliating](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Erotic_humiliation), or [non-consensual](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rape_fantasy) sex.[^cite_note-256]

Research has also shown that a significant portion of viewers of gay male pornography are heterosexual men, with a 2018 study by [YouPorn](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/YouPorn) revealing that around a quarter of straight men report watching gay porn at least occasionally.[^cite_note-257][^cite_note-258]

A study of Austrian adults found that men consume pornography more frequently than women. The intent for consumption may vary, with men being more likely to use pornography as a stimulant for sexual arousal during solitary sexual activity, while women are more likely to use pornography as a source of information or entertainment, and rather prefer using it together with a partner to enhance [sexual stimulation](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sexual_stimulation) during partnered sexual activity.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEKomlenacHochleitner2022-16] Studies have found that [sexual functioning](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sexual_function) defined as "a person's ability to respond sexually or to experience sexual pleasure" is greater in women who consume pornography frequently than in women who do not. No such association was noticed in men.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEKomlenacHochleitner2022-16] Women who consume pornography are more likely to know about their own sexual interests and desires, and in turn be willing and able to communicate them during partnered sexual activity, it has been reported that in women the ability to communicate their sexual preferences is associated with greater sexual satisfaction for themselves.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEKomlenacHochleitner2022-16] Pornographic material is found to expand the sexual repertoire in women by making them learn new rewarding sexual behaviors such as [clitoral](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Clitoral) stimulation and enhance their overall "sexual flexibility".[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEKomlenacHochleitner2022-16] Women who consume pornography frequently are more easily aroused during partnered sex and are more likely to engage in [oral sex](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Oral_sex) compared to the women who do not view pornography.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEKomlenacHochleitner2022-16] Women users of pornography had reported (almost 50%) to have had engaged in [cunnilingus](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cunnilingus), which research suggests is related to [female orgasm](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Human_female_sexuality#Orgasm), and to have had experienced orgasms more frequently than women who do not use pornography (87% vs. 64%).[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEFritzMalicFuPaul2022-239] Most people, probably do not consider pornography use by a partner as indulging in infidelity.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTELeith2014-259]

A 2024 [Economic and Social Research Institute](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Economic_and_Social_Research_Institute) (ESRI) study found that pornography consumption among 20-year-olds was highly gendered, with 64% of young men reporting that they watched pornography compared to 13% of young women, indicating that young men were nearly five times more likely to consume pornography than young women. Researchers have attributed higher rates of pornography consumption among young men to a combination of various factors, including higher average sexual curiosity and [libido](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Libido), gendered social norms that are more permissive of [male sexual behaviour](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Human_male_sexuality), greater stigma surrounding female pornography use, and differences in patterns of internet and media consumption.[^cite_note-260]

### By education level

A two year long survey (2018–2020) conducted to assess the role of pornography in the lives of highly educated medical university students, with median age of 24, in [Germany](11867.md) found that pornography served as an inspiration for many students in their [sex life](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sex_life).[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEJahnenZengKronMeissner2022-135] Pornography use among students was higher in males than in females, among the male students those who did not cheat on their partner or contracted an [STI](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sexually_transmitted_infection) were found to be more frequent consumers of pornography. Although pornography use was more common among men, associations between pornography use and sexuality were more apparent in women.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEJahnenZengKronMeissner2022-135] Among the female students, those who reported to be satisfied with their physical appearance have consumed three times as much pornography than the female students who had reported to be dissatisfied with their body. A feeling of physical inadequacy was found to be a restraining factor in the consumption of pornography. Female students who consume pornography more often had reported to have had multiple sexual partners.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEJahnenZengKronMeissner2022-135] Both female and male students who enjoyed the experience of [anal intercourse](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Anal_intercourse) in their life were reported to be frequent consumers of pornography. Sexual content depicting bondage, domination, or violence was consumed by only a minority of 10%. More sexual openness and less sexual anxiety was observed in students who regularly consumed pornography. No association was noticed between regular pornography use and experience of sexual dissatisfaction in either female or male students. This finding was in concurrence with another finding from a [longitudinal study](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Longitudinal_study), which demonstrated most pornography consumers differentiate pornographic sex from real partnered sex and do not experience diminishing satisfaction with their sex life.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEJahnenZengKronMeissner2022-135]

### By region

A vast majority of men and considerable number of women in the US use porn.[^cite_note-262][^cite_note-FOOTNOTEGrubbsPerryGrant_WeinandyKraus2021-263] A study in 2008 found that among University students aged 18 to 26 located in six college sites across the United States, 67% of young men and 49% of young women approved pornography viewing, with nearly 9 out of 10 men (87%) and 31% women reportedly using pornography.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTECarrollPadilla-WalkerNelsonOlson2008-264] [The Huffington Post](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Huffington_Post) reported in 2013 that porn websites registered higher number of visitors than Netflix, Amazon, and Twitter combined.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEKleinman2013-265][^cite_note-FOOTNOTEThousand2011-266] A 2014 poll, which asked Americans when they had "last intentionally looked at pornography", elicited a result that 46% of men and 16% of women in the age group of 18–39 did so in the past week.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEWeitzer20237-241] A 2016 study reported that about 70% of men and 34% of women in romantic relationships use pornography annually.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTELawlessKarantzasKnox2023-18] [Gallup](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gallup,_Inc.) poll surveys conducted over the years 2011 to 2018 noted a gradual increase in the acceptance rates of pornography among the general American public.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEDugan2018-267]

Since the late 1960s, attitudes towards pornography have become more positive in [Nordic countries](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Nordic_countries); in Sweden and Finland the consumption of pornography has increased over the years.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTETræenToril_SørheimHein2006-268] A 2006 study of [Norwegian](21241.md) adults found that over 80% of the respondents used pornography at some point in their lives, a difference of 20% was observed between men and women in their respective use.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEWeitzer20236–7-269] A 2015 study in [Finland](10577.md) noted that 75% of the 30-40-y.o. women and above 90% of the 30-40-y.o. men found porn "very exciting". Of those who had watched porn during the latest year, 71% of the 18-24-y.o. women, almost 60% of the 18-49-y.o. women, and a tenth of 65+ women did so; among men, the numbers were above 90% of the men under 50-y.o., 3/4 of the 18-64-y.o. and most of the 65+ y.o.; the numbers were quickly increasing, particularly for women, partially due to increased masturbation.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEKontula2017-270]

In 2012 and 2013, interviews with large number of Australians revealed that in the past year 63% of men and 20% of women had viewed pornography.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEWeitzer20236–7-269]

A 2020 Egyptian study surveying 15,027 individuals in [Arab countries](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Arab_countries) noted a prevalence of pornography use "nearly similar to Danish, German, and American ones".[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEEljawadSe'EdaGhozyEl-Qushayri2021-271]

In 2021, it was estimated that in [modern countries](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Developed_country), 46–74% of men and 16–41% of women are regular users of pornography.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEZattoniGülSoligoMorlacco2021-235] In 2022, a national survey in [Japan](15573.md), of men and women aged 20 to 69 revealed that 76% of men and 29% of women had used pornography as part of their sexual activity.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEOkabeIto2022-272] A 2023 study reported that in [Netherlands](21148.md), young men who watched porn in the previous six months ranged between 65% (13–15-y.o.) to 96% (22–24-y.o.), and among young women between 22% (13–15-y.o.) to 75% (22–24-y.o.).[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEOldenhofKraanBeekKuipers2024166-273]
