The legal status of pornography varies widely from country to country.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEDesmond201194-274][^cite_note-FOOTNOTEJoseph_BehunOwens2019102-275] Regulating [hardcore pornography](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hardcore_pornography) is more common than regulating [softcore pornography](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Softcore_pornography).[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEKaliski2007271-276] [Child pornography](25211885.md) is illegal in almost all countries,[^cite_note-FOOTNOTERodriguez2011458-277][^cite_note-FOOTNOTEClingbine201692-278] and some countries have restrictions on [rape pornography](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rape_pornography) and [zoophilic pornography](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Zoophilic_pornography).[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEClingbine201692-278]

[Pornography in the United States](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pornography_in_the_United_States) is legal provided it does not depict minors, and is not obscene.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEWeitzer20237-241] The [community standards](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Community_standards), as indicated in the [Supreme Court decision](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Miller_v._California#Supreme_Court_decision), of the 1973 [Miller v. California](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Miller_v._California) case determine what constitutes as "obscene".[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEWeitzer20237-241] The US courts do not have jurisdiction over content produced in other countries, but anyone distributing it in the US is liable to prosecution under the same community standards.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEWeitzer20237-241] As the courts consider community standards foremost in deciding any obscenity charge, the changing nature of community standards over the course of time and place makes instances of prosecution limited.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEEgan2000-122][^cite_note-FOOTNOTEFRONTLINE2002-123]

In the United States, a person receiving unwanted commercial mail that they deem pornographic (or otherwise offensive) may obtain a [Prohibitory Order](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Prohibitory_Order).[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEWileyRiveraWadlow2004152-279] Many online sites require the user to tell the website they are a certain age and no other age verification is required.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEFerguson2013148-280] A total of 16 states and the Republican Party have passed resolutions declaring pornography a "public health" threat.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEWeitzer2023376-281] These resolutions are symbolic and do not put any restrictions but are made to sway the public opinion on pornography. The notion of pornography as a threat to public health is not supported by any international health organization.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEWeitzer2023376-281]

The [adult film industry regulations](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Adult_film_industry_regulations) in California requires that all performers in pornographic films use condoms. However, the use of condoms in pornography is rare.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEDIR2022-282] As porn does better financially when actors are without condoms many companies film in other states.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTENetflix2015-283] Twitter is the popular [social media](5897742.md) platform used by the performers in porn industry as it does not censor content unlike Instagram and Facebook.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTENetflix2015-283][^cite_note-FOOTNOTEKopp20209-284]

[Pornography in Canada](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pornography_in_Canada), as in the US, criminalizes the "production, distribution, or possession" of materials that are deemed obscene. Obscenity, in the Canadian context, is defined as "the undue exploitation of sex" provided it is connected to images of "crime, horror, cruelty, or violence".[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEWeitzer20238-285] As to what is considered "undue" is decided by the courts, which assess the community standards in deciding whether exposure to the given material may result in any harm, with harm defined as "predisposing people to act in an anti-social manner".[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEWeitzer20238-285]



In May 2000, the sale of hardcore pornographic videos was legalized in licensed sex shops across the United Kingdom.

[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEBBC2000-286]

[Pornography in the United Kingdom](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pornography_in_the_United_Kingdom) does not have the concept of community standards.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEWeitzer20238-285] Following the highly publicized [murder of Jane Longhurst](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Murder_of_Jane_Longhurst), the UK government in 2009 criminalized the possession of what it terms as "[extreme pornography](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Extreme_pornography)".[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEMOJ2008-287][^cite_note-FOOTNOTEJackman2012-288] The courts decide whether any material is legally extreme or not, conviction for penalty include fines or incarceration up to three years.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEWeitzer20238-285] Content banned includes representations that are considered "grossly offensive, disgusting, or otherwise of an obscene character".[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEWeitzer20238-285] While there are no restrictions on depiction of male ejaculation, any depiction of [female ejaculation](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Female_ejaculation) in pornography is completely banned in the UK,[^cite_note-FOOTNOTESaul2014-289] as well as in Australia.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEFae2010-290]

In most of [Southeast Asia](28741.md), [Middle East](19323.md), and [China](5405.md), the production, distribution or possession of pornography is illegal and outlawed.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEWeitzer2023376-281] In [Russia](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pornography_laws_by_region#Russia) and Ukraine, [webcam modeling](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Webcam_model) is allowed provided it contains no explicit performances; in other parts of the world commercial webcamming is banned as a form of pornography.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEWeitzer2023376-281]

Disseminating pornography to a minor is generally illegal.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEClingbine201692-278] There are various measures to restrict minors' access to pornography,[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEClingbine201692-278] including protocols for pornographic stores.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEClingbine201692-278]



Entry into pornographic stores is usually allowed for only adults.

Pornography can infringe into basic human rights of those involved, especially when [sexual consent](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sexual_consent) was not obtained. [Revenge porn](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Revenge_porn) is a phenomenon where disgruntled sexual partners release images or video footage of intimate sexual activity of their partners, usually on the Internet, without authorization or consent of the individuals involved.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTECitronFranks2014-291] In many countries there has been a demand to make such activities specifically illegal carrying higher punishments than mere breach of privacy, or image rights, or circulation of prurient material.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTELevendowski2014-292][^cite_note-FOOTNOTECarter2021-293] As a result, some jurisdictions have enacted specific laws against "revenge porn".[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEBBC3_December_2014-294]

### What is not pornography

In the US, a July 2014 criminal case decision in [Massachusetts](1645518.md) — Commonwealth v. Rex, 469 Mass. 36 (2014),[^cite_note-FOOTNOTESpina2014-295] made a legal determination as to what was not to be considered "pornography" and in this particular case "child pornography".[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEKernes2014b-296] It was determined that photographs of naked children that were from sources such as [National Geographic](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/National_Geographic) magazine, a sociology textbook, and a [nudist](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Nudist) catalog were not considered pornography in Massachusetts even while in the possession of a convicted and (at the time) incarcerated [sex offender](2957039.md).[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEKernes2014b-296]

Drawing the line depends on time, place and context. Occidental mainstream culture has been increasingly getting "[pornified](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pornification)" (i.e. influenced by pornographic themes, with mainstream films often including [unsimulated sexual](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Unsimulated_sex) acts).[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEAucoin2006-297] Since the very definition of pornography is subjective, material that is considered erotic or even religious in one society may be denounced as pornography in another.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEJenkins2023-39] When European travellers visited India in the 19th century, they were dismayed at the religious representation of sexuality on the Hindu temples and deemed them as pornographic. Similarly many films and television programs that are unobjectionable in contemporary Western societies are labeled as "pornography" in Muslim societies.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEJenkins2023-39]

### Copyright status

In the United States, some courts have applied US [copyright](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Copyright) protection to pornographic materials.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEGoussé2012-298] Some courts have held that copyright protection effectively applies to works, whether they are obscene or not,[^cite_note-299] but not all courts have ruled the same way.[^cite_note-300] The copyright protection rights of pornography in the United States has again been challenged as late as February 2012.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEGoussé2012-298]

## STIs prevention and safer sex practices

See also: [Sexually transmitted infections in the pornography industry](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sexually_transmitted_infections_in_the_pornography_industry)



Hygiene products at a porn film set in [San Fernando Valley](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/San_Fernando_Valley), Los Angeles

Performers working for [pornographic film studios](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pornographic_film_studios) undergo regular testing for [sexually transmitted infections](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sexually_transmitted_infection) (STIs) every two weeks.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTETarrant2016133-301] They have to test negative for: [HIV](14170.md), [trichomoniasis](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Trichomoniasis), [chlamydia](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Chlamydia), [gonorrhea](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gonorrhea), [syphilis](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Syphilis), and [hepatitis B](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hepatitis_B) and [C](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hepatitis_C) before showing up on a set and are then inspected for sores on their mouths, hands, and genitals before commencing work. The industry believes this method of testing to be a viable practice for safer sex as its medical consultants claim that since 2004, about 350,000 pornographic scenes have been filmed without [condoms](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Condoms) and HIV has not been transmitted even once because of performance on [set](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Set_\(film_and_TV_scenery\)).[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEMcNeil_Jr.2012-302][^cite_note-FOOTNOTEHay2019-303] However, some studies suggest that adult film performers have high rates of chlamydia or gonorrhea infection, and many of these cases may be missed by industry screening because these bacteria can colonize many sites on the body.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTERodriguez-HartChitaleRiggGoldstein2012-106][^cite_note-FOOTNOTEGoldsteinSteinbergAynalemKerndt2011-107]

In the initial years, studios assessed performers suitability on the results from their blood and urine tests. According to a 2019 study by the [American College of Emergency Physicians](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/American_College_of_Emergency_Physicians), swab tests offer better insight than urine samples for detecting bacterial STIs like chlamydia and gonorrhea.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEDeVille2023-304] Performers such as Cherie DeVille have emphasized swab tests for safer sex.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEDeVille2023-304] According to performer [Angela White](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Angela_White), studios will not allow them to work unless they are completely clean, insisting on performers testing regularly, she said "So for me, because I work so much, I'm testing every 12 days – and that is a full sweep of STIs such as chlamydia, gonorrhoea, syphilis, HIV and trichomoniasis. We're doing throat swabs, vaginal swabs and anal swabs."[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEPoposki2024-305] [Allan Ronald](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Allan_Ronald), a Canadian doctor and [HIV/AIDS](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/HIV/AIDS) specialist who did groundbreaking studies on the transmission of STIs among prostitutes in Africa, said there's no doubt about the efficiency of the testing method, but he felt a little uncomfortable: "because it's giving the wrong message — that you can have multiple sex partners without condoms — but I can't say it doesn't work."[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEMcNeil_Jr.2012-302][^cite_note-FOOTNOTEHay2019-303]

Relatedly, it has been found that individuals who received little [sex education](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sex_education) or perceive pornography as a source of information about sex are less apt to use condoms in their own [sex life](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sex_life), making themselves more susceptible to contract STIs.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEWrightHerbenickPaul2020-306][^cite_note-FOOTNOTEWrightSunSteffen2018-307] In 2020—the US National Sex Education Standards—released recommendations to incorporate "porn literacy" to students from grade 6 to 12 as part of [sex education in the US](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sex_education_in_the_United_States).[^cite_note-FOOTNOTERothman2021231-308]

Veteran performer and former nurse [Nina Hartley](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Nina_Hartley), who has a degree in [nursing](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Nursing), stated that the amount of time involved in shooting a scene can be very long, and with condoms in place it becomes a painful proposition as their usage is uncomfortable despite the use of [lube](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Personal_lubricant), causes [friction burn](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Friction_burn), and opens up [lesions](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Skin_condition#Lesions) in the genital mucosa.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEMcNeil_Jr.2012-302][^cite_note-FOOTNOTEWebber2021-309] Advocating the testing method for performers, Hartley said, "Testing works for us, and condoms work for outsiders."[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEWebber2021-309]

> "We're tested every fourteen days. That is literally twenty-three more times than the average American. If that person makes it to their yearly physical. I have met tons of people that haven't been to the doctor in years. That scares me because they have no idea what their status is.... I don't hook up with people outside of the porn industry because I'm terrified. And I'm not the only one. There's many performers that know: if you go out into the wild, you will come back with something." — Ash Hollywood (Porn actress).[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEWebber2021-309]

Emphasizing that performers in the industry take necessary precautions like [PrEP](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/PrEP) and are at lower risk to contract HIV than most sexually active persons outside the industry,[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEHay2019-303] many prominent female performers have vehemently opposed regulatory measures like [Measure B](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Measure_B) that sought to make the use of condoms mandatory in pornographic films. Professional female performers have called the use of condoms on a daily basis at work an occupational hazard as they cause micro-tears, friction burn, swelling, and yeast infections, which altogether, they say, makes them more susceptible to contract STIs.[^cite_note-312]

## Views on pornography



A [sex positive](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sex_positive) poster in Amsterdam (2021)

Pornography has been vouched to provide a safe outlet for sexual desires that may not be satisfied within relationships and be a facilitator of sexual fulfillment in people who cannot or do not want to have real-life partners.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEMcNair201310-191] Pornography is viewed by people in general for various reasons; varying from a need to enrich their sexual arousal, to facilitate [orgasm](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Orgasm), as an aid for [masturbation](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Masturbation), learn about sexual techniques, reduce stress, alleviate boredom, enjoy themselves, see representation of people like themselves, know their [sexual orientation](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sexual_orientation), improve their romantic relationships, or simply because their partner wants them to.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEFritzMalicFuPaul2022-239]

Pornography is noted for engrossing people "on more than masturbatory levels".[^cite_note-FOOTNOTELubey2022214-313] [Aesthetic](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Aesthetic) philosophers argue whether pornographic representations can be considered as expressions of [art](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Art).[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEMaesLevinson20122–12-314] Pornography has been equated with journalism as both offer a view into the unknown or the hidden aspects. French philosopher [Michel Foucault](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Michel_Foucault) remarked that, "it is in pornography that we find information about the hidden, the forbidden and the taboo".[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEMcNair201316-315]

Scholars such as [Linda Williams](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Linda_Williams_\(film_scholar\)), [Jennifer Nash](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jennifer_Nash), and [Tim Dean](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tim_Dean) believe pornography "is a form of thinking", comprised with ideas that are way more reflective about sexuality and gender than what the creators or consumers of pornography intend.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTELubey2022214-313] Pornography has been referred by people as a means to explore their sexuality. People have reported porn being helpful in learning about [human sexuality](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Human_sexuality) in general.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEKomlenacHochleitner2022-16] Studies recommend clinical practitioners to use pornography as an instruction aid to show their clients new and alternative sexual behaviors as part of [psychosexual therapy](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Psychosexual_disorder).[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEKomlenacHochleitner2022-16] British psychologist, [Oliver James](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Oliver_James_\(psychologist\)), known for his work on 'happiness', stated that "a high proportion of men use porn as a distraction or to reduce stress ... It serves an [anti-depressant](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Anti-depressant) purpose for the unhappy."[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEMcNair2013123-316] British-American novelist, [Salman Rushdie](28722.md) opined that pornography presence in society is "a kind of standard-bearer for freedom, even civilisation".[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEBaxterBrooks2004-317]

As per evaluation by medical professionals, pornography can neither be good nor bad as it does not endorse or advocate a single set of [values](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Values) regarding sex.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEVillinesLitner2020-318] As such, individuals may introspect their own values with regards to sex while evaluating pornography.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEVillinesLitner2020-318] The relationship between pornography and its audience is found to be complex. While many users reported their use to have had positive [effects](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Effects_of_pornography), others especially women were found to be troubled with [body image](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Body_image) issues, the cause of which is attributed to the unrealistic image of "beauty" that pornography portrays.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEVillinesLitner2020-318] The increasing prevalence of alleged beauty enhancing procedures such as [breast augmentation](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Breast_augmentation) and [labiaplasty](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Labiaplasty) among the common populace has been attributed to the popularity of pornography.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTETarrant2016136-319]

Data from pornographic websites regarding the viewing habits of people is studied by academics to analyze the sexual preferences and mating choices.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTESalmonFisherBurch2020550–551-320] More often men look for women who have larger chest and hips, with a smaller [waist–hip ratio](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Waist%E2%80%93hip_ratio).[^cite_note-FOOTNOTESalmonFisherBurch2020552-321] Women are found to prefer men who are taller, stronger, appear highly masculine, and are in roles that can provide resources while being protective (CEO, doctor, athlete, lawmen).[^cite_note-FOOTNOTESalmonFisherBurch2020553-322]

Studies on harmful effects of pornography include finding any potential influence of pornography on rape, [domestic violence](18978563.md), [sexual dysfunction](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sexual_dysfunction), difficulties with sexual relationships, and [child sexual abuse](18721790.md).[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEBrown2017-323] A [longitudinal study](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Longitudinal_study) had ascertained that pornography use cannot be a perpetrating factor in [intimate partner violence](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Intimate_partner_violence).[^cite_note-ipviolence-4][^cite_note-FOOTNOTEHatchEsplinAaronDowdle2020-324] A 2020 study that analyzed depictions in video-pornography found that normative sexual behaviors (e.g., vaginal intercourse, fellatio) were the most commonly depicted, while depictions of extreme acts of violence and rape were very rare.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTECarrotteDavisLim2020-325] There is no clear evidence to assume that pornography is a cause of rape.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEHowitt2022166-326] Several studies conclude that liberalization of porn in society may be associated with decreased rates of rape and sexual violence, while others have suggested no effect, or are inconclusive.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEKutchinsky1992-327][^cite_note-FOOTNOTED'Amato2006-328][^cite_note-FOOTNOTEDiamond1999-329] No correlation has been found between pornography use and the practice of [sexual consent](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sexual_consent) or lack thereof.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEMcKeeLitsouByronIngham202246-330]

Mental health experts are divided over the issue of pornography use being a problem for people.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEMcKeeLitsouByronIngham2022102-331] While some [literature reviews](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Literature_reviews) suggest pornography use can be addictive, insufficient evidence exists to draw conclusions.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEKrausVoonPotenza2016-332][^cite_note-FOOTNOTEKühnGallinat2016-333][^cite_note-FOOTNOTEBrandYoungLaierWölfling2016-334] According to [David J. Ley](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/David_J._Ley), [pornography addiction](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pornography_addiction) has been "an area of substantial, protracted controversy and debate". Ley argued pornography doesn't effect an adult brain or body in the way alcohol or drugs do, he said "An alcoholic going [cold turkey](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cold_turkey) can have [seizures](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Seizures) and die because their brain has become physiologically dependent on the alcohol, but no one has ever had seizures or died from not getting to watch porn when they want to."[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEAustin2024-186] Scholars note that general pornography use has no implication on public health as it does not meet the definition of a [public health crisis](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Public_health#Definitions_and_purposes).[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEFritzMalicFuPaul2022-239] A study found neural mechanisms associated with [behavioral addictions](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Behavioral_addiction).[^cite_note-y845-335]

[Neuroscience](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Neuroscience) has noted that minds of the young are in developmental stages and exposure to emotionally charged material such as pornography would likely have a higher impact on them compared to adults, and has suggested caution while enabling potential access to such material.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTESegal2014-336] [Opposition to pornography](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Opposition_to_pornography) use is associated with sexual satisfaction,[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEWrightBridgesSunEzzell2018-337] gender violence,[^cite_note-FOOTNOTELimCarrotteHellard2016-338] and marital quality (wives watching pornography more frequently scored much better than the rest).[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEPerry2017-339] Some issues of [doxing](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Doxing) and [revenge porn](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Revenge_porn) had been linked to a few pornography websites.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEColeMaiberg2019a-340][^cite_note-FOOTNOTECole2020a-341][^cite_note-FOOTNOTEBroster2019-342] Since the mid-2010s, [deepfake pornography](62590682.md) has become an issue of concern.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEColeMaiberg2019b-205]

### Feminist outlook

Main article: [Feminist views on pornography](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Feminist_views_on_pornography)

Feminist movements in the late 1970s and 1980s dealt with the issues of pornography and sexuality in debates that are referred to as the "[sex wars](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sex_wars)".[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEHester201419-343] While some [feminist groups](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Feminist_movements_and_ideologies#Groupings) seek to abolish pornography believing it to be harmful, other feminist groups oppose censorship efforts insisting it is benign.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTESpeedMacDonaldParksDoucette2021-17] A large scale study of data from the [General Social Survey](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/General_Social_Survey) (2010–2018) refuted the argument that pornography is inherently anti-woman or anti-feminist and that it drives [sexism](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sexism). The study did not find a relationship between "pornography viewing" and "pornography tolerance" with higher sexism—a posit that was held by some feminists; it instead found higher pornography consumption and pornography tolerance among men to be associated with their greater support for [gender equality](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gender_equality). The study concluded that "pornography is more likely to be about the sex rather than the sexism".[^cite_note-FOOTNOTESpeedMacDonaldParksDoucette2021-17]

People who supported regulated pornography expressed lesser attitudes of sexism than people who sought to abolish pornography. Notably, non-feminists are found more likely to support a ban on pornography than feminists. Many feminists, both male and female, have reflected that the effects of pornography on society are neutral.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTESpeedMacDonaldParksDoucette2021-17] Adult users of pornography were found more egalitarian than nonusers, they are more likely to hold favorable attitudes towards women in positions of power and in workplaces outside home than the nonusers.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEKohutBaerWatts2016-344]

#### Criticism

A 2016 study authored by [Black feminists](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Black_feminists) criticized the American adult entertainment industry for alleged omission and exclusion of [Black women](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Black_women) in pornographic representations, particularly in the interracial genres. As pornography becomes a kind of manual on how bodies in pleasure can look, and is "one of the few places where we see our bodies--and other people's bodies," it becomes imperative on pornography to represent "variety of forms", stated the feminist scholars.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEChude-SokeiCruzMusserNash201657-345] Anti-pornography feminists argue that aesthetics of pornography demote Black women with undertones of racism.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTERothman202163-346] Gender studies scholars [Mireille Miller-Young](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mireille_Miller-Young) and [Jennifer Christine Nash](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jennifer_Christine_Nash), in their writings on [intersectionality](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Intersectionality) of race and pornography, noted that Black people have been depicted as being [hypersexual](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hypersexual) and Black women—more objectified.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTERothman202164-347] The scholars also noted major discrepancies in pay rates of the performers, White women have historically made 75 percent more per scene and sometimes still make 50 percent more compared to Black women.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTELieberman2020-348]

Feminist resentment about pornography tend to focus on two concerns: that pornography depicts violence and aggression, and that pornography objectifies women.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTERothman202164-347] Multiple analyses of pornographic videos found that Women have been overwhelmingly at the receiving end of aggression from male performers; with the reaction of Women being either positive or neutral towards aggression, which is at odds considering a report that found only 14.2% of US adult women find pain during sex as appealing.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTERothman202164-347] Two studies in the 1990s found that Black women were the targets of aggression and faced more violence from both Black and White men than did White women.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTERothman202163-346] However, more recent research from 2018 found that Black women were the least likely group of women to suffer nonconsensual aggression and are more likely to receive affection from their male partners.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEShorGolriz201910-349] While Black men engaged in fewer intimate behaviors than White men; White women were found more likely to experience violence during sexual activity with White men than with Black men.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTERothman202163-346]

Concerning Asian women, a 2016 study based on a sample of 3053 videos from Xvideos.com, found that in the 170 videos of the Asian women category, there was much less aggression, less objectification, but also the women had less agency.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTERothman202163-346] However, another study found that in a sample of 172 videos from Pornhub, the 25+ videos of the Asian/Japanese category had considerably more aggression than those of other categories.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEShorGolriz201911-350] A 2002 study of "internet rape sites" found that among the 56 clear pictures they found, 34 had Asian women, and nearly half the sites had either an image or a text reference to an Asian woman.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEGossettByrne2002703-351] Findings on depictions of Asian women in pornography are inconsistent in scientific literature.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEMillerMcBain2022-352][^cite_note-FOOTNOTERothman202163-346]

The prevalence of aggression in pornography appears to be changing. A 2018 study of popular videos on Pornhub found that segments of aggression towards women are fewer now, and they have reduced gradually over the past decade with viewers preferring content where women genuinely experience pleasure.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEShorSeida201916–28-353]

#### Anti-pornography

Main article: [Opposition to pornography](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Opposition_to_pornography)



A [caricature](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Caricature) on "The great epidemic of pornography". French illustration (c. 1880 – c. 1900 CE).

Prominent anti-pornography feminists such as [Andrea Dworkin](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Andrea_Dworkin) and [Catharine MacKinnon](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Catharine_MacKinnon) argue that all pornography is demeaning to women, or that it contributes to [violence against women](3634424.md)–both in its production and in its consumption. The production of pornography, they argue, entails the physical, psychological, or economic coercion of the women who perform in it. They charged that pornography eroticizes the domination, humiliation, and coercion of women, while reinforcing sexual and cultural attitudes that are complicit in rape and [sexual harassment](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sexual_harassment).[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEShrage2004-354][^cite_note-FOOTNOTEMacKinnon1989a195–214-355][^cite_note-FOOTNOTEPBS1995-356]

Other sex work exclusionary feminists have insisted that pornography presents a severely distorted image of [sexual consent](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sexual_consent), and it reinforces sexual myths like: women are readily available–and desire to engage in sex at any time–with any man–on men's terms–and always respond positively to men's advances.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEJeffries2006-357]

#### Pro-pornography

In contrast to the objections, other feminist scholars "ranging from [Betty Friedan](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Betty_Friedan) and [Kate Millett](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kate_Millett) to [Karen DeCrow](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Karen_DeCrow), [Wendy Kaminer](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wendy_Kaminer) and [Jamaica Kincaid](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jamaica_Kincaid)" have supported the right to consume pornography.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTECarol1995-358] The anti-porn feminist stranglehold began to loosen when [sex-positive feminists](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sex-positive_feminists) like [Susie Bright](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Susie_Bright), performers [Nina Hartley](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Nina_Hartley), and [Candida Royalle](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Candida_Royalle) affirmed the rights of women to consume and produce porn.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEAngelHudsonJonesGlaser2018-359]

The works of [Camille Paglia](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Camille_Paglia) established that westerners have been "[pagan celebrants](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Paganism)" for long and pornography has been an inseparable part of [western culture](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Western_culture).[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEAngelHudsonJonesGlaser2018-359] [Wendy McElroy](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wendy_McElroy) has noted that both feminism and pornography are mutually related, with both thriving in environments of tolerance, and both repressed anytime regulations are placed on sexual expression.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEMcElroy199536-360] Societies where pornography and sexual expression is prohibited are more likely to be the places where women are often subjected to violence and sexual abuse.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEMcNair201389-361]

> Women's rights are far stronger in societies with liberal attitudes to sex – think of conservative countries such as Afghanistan, Yemen or China, and the place of women there. And yet, anti-porn campaigners neglect such issues entirely. A recent study by the US department of justice compared the four states that had highest broadband access and found there was a 27 per cent decrease in rape and attempted rape, and the four with the lowest had a 53 per cent increase over the same period.

— [Anna Span](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Anna_Span)

[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEMcNair201389-361]

#### Rise of feminist pornography

The [lesbian feminist](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lesbian_feminist) movement of the 1980s is considered a seminal moment for the women in porn industry as more women entered into the developmental side, allowing women to gear porn more towards women as they knew what women wanted, both from the perspective of actresses as well as the female audience. This involved making [lesbian pornography](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lesbian_pornography) that is not merely geared towards heterosexual males—a change considered good, as for a long time the porn industry had been directed by men for men.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEZiv2014-362]

Furthermore, the advent of the [VCR](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/VCR), [home video](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Home_video), and affordable [video cameras](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Video_cameras) allowed for the possibility of [feminist pornography](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Feminist_pornography).[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEComellaErickson201379–96-363] Feminist porn directors are interested in challenging representations of men and women, as well as in providing sexually-empowering imagery that features many kinds of bodies.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEComellaErickson2013320–328-364] [Angela White](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Angela_White) started her own production company, AWG Entertainment, in which she has complete creative control over the content—from her partners, to the location, costumes, and the "vibe" of the video, "I am a feminist, so what I create is feminist, and I produce ethical porn, which is when everything is consensual", she said.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTETopsfield2022-365]

Women are more likely to consume porn that is "female-centered" and feature acts such as [cunnilingus](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cunnilingus). Female-centric porn is mostly made by women, in these works the initiation of sexual activity is done by the female.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTESalmonFisherBurch2020554-366] [Porn for women](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Porn_for_women) is identified by factors like greater attention on "sensual surroundings" and "soft focus camerawork", rather than on explicit depiction of sexual activity, making the productions more warm and humane compared to the traditional porn made for heterosexual men.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEMcNair201318-367]

> &#x20;"If feminists define pornography, per se, as the enemy, the result will be to make a lot of women ashamed of their sexual feelings and afraid to be honest about them. And the last thing women need is more sexual shame, guilt, and hypocrisy—this time served up by feminism" — [Ellen Willis](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ellen_Willis).[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEHester201424-368]

#### Pay rates

Porn industry has been noted for being one of the few industries where women enjoy a power advantage in the workplace. Female performers can often dictate which male actors they will and will not work with. Porn is one of the few contemporary occupations where the [pay gap](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gender_pay_gap) operates in the favor of women. The average actress makes fifty to a hundred per cent more money than her male counterpart.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEFaludi199565–66-369][^cite_note-FOOTNOTEMorris2016-370][^cite_note-FOOTNOTETopsfield2022-365]

### Psychological perspective

[Psychologists](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Psychologist) consider pornography to be of particular relevance in the study of [intimate relationships](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Intimate_relationship) and the development of [adolescent sexuality](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Adolescent_sexuality).[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEGrubbsKraus2021-371] Mainstream [psychology](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Psychology) is mostly concerned with the study of [effects of pornography](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Effects_of_pornography), while [critical psychology](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Critical_psychology) and [applied psychology](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Applied_psychology) is engaged in more nuanced and academic study of pornography.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEBarker2014-372] Problematic pornography use is assessed in [clinical psychology](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Clinical_psychology).[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEGrubbsKraus2021-371]

A 2013 study refuted the notion that porn actresses have higher rates of psychological problems than regular women. The study compared 177 porn actresses with regular women of similar age, ethnicity, and marital status, and found that the porn actresses had "higher levels of self-esteem, positive feelings, social support, sexual satisfaction, and spirituality" compared to the regular women.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEGriffithMitchellHartAdams2013621–632-373]

#### Psychoanalyst views

In [analytical psychology](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Analytical_psychology), humans sexual and [religious-spiritual](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_religions_and_spiritual_traditions) instincts are considered tightly associated with each other, with both sharing a common objective, which, as [Carl Jung](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Carl_Jung) acknowledged, is the striving of the [psyche](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Psyche_\(psychology\)) for "wholeness".[^cite_note-FOOTNOTETricarico201861-374] The psyche of a person is understood to be differentiated, being made-up of [traits](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Trait_theory) that are [feminine](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Feminine) and [masculine](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Masculine) in nature.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTETricarico201868-375] According to Jung, this differentiation allowed the formation of opposite polarities that make "[consciousness](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Consciousness) possible".[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEPettis201462-376] According to psychologist and author Giorgio Tricarico, as an individual moves through various life experiences, their psyche approaches wholeness or the state of "[non-differentiated](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Nondualism)"—a realm of higher nondual consciousness—considered belonging to the sacred or divine.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTETricarico201857–78-377] In [Hindu tantric](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tantra#Hindu_tantra) view, the guiding image of a male and a female conjoined in sexual intercourse represent the embodiment of nondual consciousness.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEMadden20141777–1779-378] Men and women, however they appear, are considered microcosmic compounds of the macrocosmic principles—[Shiva](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Shiva) (matter) and [Shakti](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Shakti) (spirit).[^cite_note-FOOTNOTECamphausen1999225-379] Shiva and Shakti, together in "perpetual union", form the nondual "[Absolute](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Absolute_\(philosophy\))".[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEJain201414–15Leeming20141646–1647Sarbacker201134,_38-380]



Shiva and Shakti in a state of "copulation" represent the flow of "erotic energy" in an individual.

[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEJain201414–15-381]

&#x20;Painting showing goddess [Kali](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kali) (a personification of Shakti) in union with [Shiva](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Shiva)—together *it* forms the [self-existent](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Existence) nondual Absolute. 19th century portrait, in [Cleveland Museum of Art](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cleveland_Museum_of_Art), U.S.

[Sigmund Freud](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sigmund_Freud) called the feminine Shakti "[libido](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Libido) that cannot be simply repressed."[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEBailey20141669-382] Self-realization or becoming aware of the " 'deep' femininity" entails dealing with the powerful sexual energy.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEPlancke2020-383] Tantric rituals like [maithuna](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Maithuna) harness the sexual energy in order to make the male and the female principles that appear seemingly opposite-arrive into unity or a harmonious whole in the "divine feminine" or "unified divine consciousness", an idea analogous with analytical psychology's *[coincidentia oppositorum](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Coincidentia_oppositorum)*.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEMadden20141777–1779-378] In classical Hindu thought, the nature of the [self](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/%C4%80tman_\(Hinduism\)) in males and females is assessed as being [androgyne](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Androgyny), with sexuality being a creative function of the divine to align the human self with its bipolar nature.[^cite_note-384] The masculine and the feminine principles of the self were identified with Shiva and Shakti, who make-up the two [sexual polarities](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sexual_polarity); by establishing a connection between the two, for the flow of erotic energy (as in an [electric circuit](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Electric_current#Conventions) between positive and negative terminals for the flow of [electric current](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Electric_current)), in one's own being—by the means of sexual stimulation—through "erotic visualization" or "ritual copulation", the self would "divest" from its body identity and realign into the "[bipolar being](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ardhanarishvara)", which, then represents a unit microcosm mirroring the nondual macrocosm; thus an individual in [being one](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Brahman#Advaita_Vedanta) with the absolute experiences *bliss*-considered as the power of the goddess (Shakti) in a tangible form.[^cite_note-385]

In the Hindu tantric view, the women who participate in union rituals, thereby enabling men to attain self-realization are regarded as shakti or the goddess, as they are believed to embody *her*.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTECamphausen1999205-386] Recognition of the deity in an objective woman is centered upon a man's acceptance of the *[subjective](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Subject_and_object_\(philosophy\))* feminine and the primacy of her desires.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEBiernacki2006200-387] Tricarico professed that modern-day pornography in its essence is a "desacralised, technological, and consumerist" equivalent of the ancient [sacred prostitution](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sacred_prostitution) – a custom that involved honoring of the sacred feminine and worship of the prostitutes as [goddess](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Goddess).[^cite_note-FOOTNOTETricarico201870–71-388] The feminine is believed to embody particular qualities of the sacred or divine more broadly and deeply than the masculine, consequently in women, the ability to incorporate [nondualistic](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Nondualism) awareness is assumed to be higher.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTETricarico201868-375] Tricarico argued that women in porn, through their performances of many sexual acts, would inadvertently approach the non-differentiated state, an effect which he called the "intimation of [hierophany](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hierophany)".[^cite_note-FOOTNOTETricarico201868-375] "Porn actresses may embody the medium to enter what used to be the realm of the sacred", he said.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTETricarico201877-389] The actresses have been likened to the "descendants of the lost goddesses" who are now offering the gift of the "[numinous](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Numinous)" to all through their performances, but are unacknowledged or devalued for their contributions.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTETricarico201877-389]

### Religious attitudes

Main article: [Religious views on pornography](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Religious_views_on_pornography)

Many [religions](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Religions) have long and vehemently opposed a wide range of sexual behaviors, as a result religious people are found highly susceptible to experience great distress in their use of pornography. Religious people who use pornography tend to feel sexually ashamed.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEVolkThomasSosinJacob2016244–259-390] [Sexual shame](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sexual_shame)—which arises from a person's perception of their self in other peoples mind, and a negative assessment of their own sexuality—is considered a powerful factor that over time governs an individual's behavior.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEVolkThomasSosinJacob2016244–259-390][^cite_note-FOOTNOTEGordon2018105–123-391] As sexuality is interwoven into one's personal [identity](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Identity_\(social_science\)), sexual shame or sexual embarrassment are found to attack the person's very sense of self.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEVolkThomasSosinJacob2016244–259-390]

When a sexual shaming event occurs, the person attributes causation to oneself resulting in self condemnation, and experience feelings of sadness, loneliness, anger, unworthiness, and rejection, along with a perceived judgment of their self by others.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEVolkThomasSosinJacob2016244–259-390] In this mental landscape, a fear arises that ones sexual self needs to be hidden. This psychological process initiates and fuels further shame and lowers one's self-esteem.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEVolkThomasSosinJacob2016244–259-390] Sexual shame constricts the "psychic space for free play with one's sexuality".[^cite_note-FOOTNOTECole2014-392] Sexual shame in people begets more [shame](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Shame), and leads to a cycle of powerlessness culminating in deepening negative emotions.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEVolkThomasSosinJacob2016244–259-390] Those who tend to feel shame easily are found to be at greater risk for [depression](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Depression_\(mood\)) and [anxiety disorders](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Anxiety_disorders).[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEKämmerer2019-393] According to clinical psychologist Gershen Kaufman, all [Sexual disorders](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sexual_dysfunction) are majorly "disorders of shame".[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEKaufman2004-394]

The cause of attributing shame to sexuality is traced back to the [biblical](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Biblical) interpretation of nakedness being shameful.[^cite_note-395] Much of the [Christian mythology](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Christian_mythology) presented sexuality as an obstacle to be surmounted in the way of salvation. The major [Abrahamic religions](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Abrahamic_religions) condemn and consider all forms of *nonmarital* and *nonreproductive* sexual pleasure as unacceptable. In [Hinduism](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hinduism), [bhoga](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bhoga) (sexual pleasure) is celebrated as a value in itself and is considered one of the two ways to [nirvana](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Nirvana#Hinduism), the other being the more demanding [yoga](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Yoga). In the Hindu tantric view, watching coitus as an act of Shiva and Shakti is believed to unfurl the [Kundalini](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kundalini), and is considered equivalent to one's engaging in [maithuna](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Maithuna) or the fifth M of the [panchamakara](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Panchamakara).[^cite_note-397] A central concept in Hinduism, [purushartha](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Purushartha), advocates pursuit of the four main goals for happiness: [dharma](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dharma) (virtue), [artha](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Artha) (riches), [kama](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kama) (pleasure), and [moksha](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Moksha) (freedom).[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEFlood199617-398] The pursuit of Kama was elaborated by the sage Vatsyayana in his treatise *[Kama Sutra](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kama_Sutra)*, which states that sexual pleasure and food are essential for the well-being of the body, and on both of them depend virtue and prosperity.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEVatsyayana1994\[httpsarchiveorgdetailsthe-complete-kama-sutra-the-first-unabridged-modern-translation-of-the-classic-ipage42mode1upviewtheater_42\]-399][^cite_note-FOOTNOTELudo1985-400] Food, despite causing indigestion sometimes, would still be consumed regularly, and so it must be with pleasure, which must be pursued with caution while eliminating unwanted or harmful effects. As no one abstains from cooking food worrying about beggars who ask for it, or restrain from sowing wheat fearing animals that destroy the crop, similarly, instructs [Vatsyayana](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Vatsyayana), that men and women acquire knowledge of Kama by the time they reach youth and pursue it even though dangers exist; and those who become accomplished in *Dharma*, *Artha*, and *Kama* would attain highest happiness in this world and hereafter.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEVatsyayana1994\[httpsarchiveorgdetailsthe-complete-kama-sutra-the-first-unabridged-modern-translation-of-the-classic-ipage43mode1upviewtheater_42-43\]-401][^cite_note-FOOTNOTELudo1985-400]

According to [the Buddha](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Buddha), happiness is of two types: one derived from "domestic life" and the other from "monastic life", and between the two, monastic kind is "superior".[^cite_note-FOOTNOTENumrich200968-402] As a result of the Buddha's effective advocacy for [monasticism](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Monasticism), in Buddhist communities, marriage and divorce remained civil matters and never acquired sacremental significance.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTENumrich200969-403] Counsel over [sex life](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sex_life) for householders was minimal, while for the monks it was extensive as in [Vinaya](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Vinaya) since all sexual behaviors were meant to be suppressed for the sake of [enlightenment](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Enlightenment_in_Buddhism).[^cite_note-FOOTNOTENumrich200968-402] The early Buddhist texts castigated women as detrimental beings. The Buddha himself said often that a woman's body is "a vessel of impurity, full of stinking filth. It is like a rotten pit ... like a toilet, with nine holes pouring all sorts of filth."[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEFaure2003\[httpsbooksgooglecombooksidRvO0vO575owCpgPA56_56\]-404] Once when it came to his notice that a monk, Suddina, transgressed celibacy with his wife for the sake of progeny, the Buddha chided him saying, "It were better for you, foolish man, that your male organ should enter the mouth of a terrible and poisonous snake, than that it should enter a woman."[^cite_note-FOOTNOTELangenberg2015278-405] Per the Buddha, all sexual desires are incompatible with [enlightenment](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Enlightenment_in_Buddhism).[^cite_note-FOOTNOTELangenberg2015278-405] In [Buddhism](3267529.md), people who even derive pleasure from watching others engage in sexual activity were relegated as [pandaka](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pandaka) (pusillanimous).[^cite_note-FOOTNOTENumrich200970-406] The Buddha said sexuality is a [fetter](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Fetter_\(Buddhism\)) that must be evaded completely and men who engage with it are "[impure](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Purity_in_Buddhism)" and will not be freed from "old age".[^cite_note-FOOTNOTENumrich200967-407] After [the Buddha](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Buddha) died in old age,[^cite_note-FOOTNOTECousins1996-408] subsequent generations of Buddhists resolved their problematic attitudes towards sex by accommodating different views.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTELangenberg2015278-405][^cite_note-FOOTNOTENumrich200968-402]

According to Indonesia's foremost Islamic preacher, [A. Gymnastiar](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Abdullah_Gymnastiar), shame is a noble emotion commanded in the Islamic scripture and was held high by the prophet, who had been quoted as saying "Faith is compiled of seventy branches... and shame is one of them." To cultivate shame in Muslims, their sexual gaze needs to be checked, as unchecked gaze is believed to be the door through which [Satan](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Satan) enters and soils the heart.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEHoesterey2016-409] In 2006, when anti-pornography protests erupted in [Indonesia](14579.md), the world's most populous [Muslim-majority country](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Islam_by_country), over the publication of the inaugural Indonesian edition of *[Playboy](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Playboy)* – Gymnastiar called for a legislation to ban pornography and embarked on a mission to shroud the state with a sense of shame, giving the slogan "the more shameful, the more faithful". During these protests, Indonesia's foremost Islamic newspaper, *[Republika](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Republika_\(Indonesian_newspaper\))*, published daily front-page editorials which featured a logo of the word pornografi crossed out with a red X. The Jakarta office of *[Playboy Indonesia](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Playboy_Indonesia)* was ransacked by members of the Islamic Defenders Front (Front Pembela Islam or FPI), and bookstore owners were threatened not to sell any issue of the magazine. Consequently, in December 2008, Indonesian lawmakers signed an [anti-pornography bill](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pornography_Law_\(Indonesia\)) into law with overwhelming political support.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEHoesterey2016-409]

Highly religious people are more likely to support policies against pornography such as censorship.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEDroubayButtersShafer2021-410] Ironically, regions with highly religious and conservative people were found to search for more pornography online.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEShultz2014-411] Religious people are prone to having obsessive thoughts regarding [sin](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sin) and punishment by God over their pornography use causing them to feel ashamed, and perceive themselves to have [pornography addiction](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pornography_addiction) while also suffering from [OCD](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sexual_obsessions#Obsessive-compulsive_disorder) related symptoms.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEDe_JongCook2021-412] A study of sexually active religious people found those who are highly spiritually matured have less shame, while those not spiritually matured have high shame.[^cite_note-FOOTNOTEKellerMollenRosen2015-413]

[^cite_note-definition-1]: &#x20;Pornography can be defined as "material \[e.g., pictures, films, videos or text] deemed sexual, given the context, that has the primary intention of sexually arousing the consumer, and is produced and distributed with the consent of all persons involved" (McDonald & Kirkman, 2019, p. 163). Central in the definition of pornography is the consent of all persons involved. Therefore, materials that were produced or distributed without the consent of at least one person involved (e.g., "revenge porn", "child pornography") were excluded from this definition (McDonald & Kirkman, 2019).<sup>[\[12\]](#cite_note-FOOTNOTEKomlenacHochleitner2022-16)</sup> Pornography is best defined as a medium, such as a picture, video, or text, that is intended to be treated as sexually arousing (Rea, \[41]). \[...] pornography is framed as an aid for sexual arousal (Parvez, \[32]).<sup>[\[13\]](#cite_note-FOOTNOTESpeedMacDonaldParksDoucette2021-17)</sup> Using pornography means to intentionally look at, watch, read, or listen to sexually arousing material (pictures, videos, films, written text or audio) which depicts nudity or explicit sexual behavior. This does not include participating in interactive sexual experiences in person or online, such as a "lap-dance" in a strip club or a live sex chat.<sup>[\[14\]](#cite_note-FOOTNOTELawlessKarantzasKnox2023-18)</sup>

[^cite_note-oldest-2]: &#x20;Anthropologist Paul Mellars of Stony Brook University in New York state says the focus on exaggerated sexual features fits with other artifacts found from the period, including phalluses carved out of bison horn and vulva inscribed on rocks. "It's sexually exaggerated to the point of being pornographic", Mellars says. "There's all this sexual symbolism bubbling up in that period. They were sex-mad." Conard used radiocarbon dates from bones and other artifacts found nearby to date the figurine. "It's at least 35,000 calendar years old, but I think it's much older than that", Conard says.<sup>[\[19\]](#cite_note-FOOTNOTEScience13_May_2009-23)</sup>

[^cite_note-use-3]: &#x20;Representative studies indicate that pornography use is a common recreational activity—equivalent with other digitally mediated behaviors (e.g., video games, social media)—with a majority of men and a sizable plurality of women reporting regular use of pornography.<sup>[\[242\]](#cite_note-FOOTNOTEGrubbsFloydKraus2023-247)</sup>

[^cite_note-ipviolence-4]: &#x20;Using a large longitudinal sample of university students (N = 892) over a three-month time lag with two waves and a cross-lagged panel design, we found that pornography use does not prospectively predict the perpetration of intimate partner violence, and that the perpetration of intimate partner violence does not prospectively predict pornography use. Further, gender does not moderate these relationships.<sup>[\[317\]](#cite_note-FOOTNOTEHatchEsplinAaronDowdle2020-324)</sup>

[^cite_note-161]: &#x20;The pornographic genre is immense, and includes an enormous variety of styles catering to an equally vast range of tastes and fetishes. Certainly, mainstream heteroporn makes up the main bulk of the genre, and is most easily accessible. As stated above, this style of porn includes highly formulaic displays of paired or group sex, enacted by bodies exhibiting a conventional gendered aesthetic, moving through various sexual positions and penetrations. Nonetheless, some forms of porn are more normative than others, and indeed not all forms of heteroporn are normative, such as 'rimming', girl-on-boy strap-on anal sex, and hard-core BDSM. Pornography also includes an endless array of different kinds of fetish, 'fat' porn, amateur porn, disabled porn, porn produced by women, queer porn, BDSM and body modification. The list of non-mainstream porn is endless and displays bodies, gender scenarios and sexual activity differently to heteronormative formulations of mainstream heteroporn.<sup>[\[141\]](#cite_note-FOOTNOTEMulholland2011-145)</sup>

[^cite_note-262]: &#x20;If estimates generated from the RIA or NFSS are more valid, then pornography use is—or perhaps has become—a common and frequent experience among men, with just under half of all men using pornography in an average week. [^cite_note-312]: &#x20;She didn't know that the dangers of it, like if the condom breaks, and that we could get more STI's with the micro-tears, and just the condoms in general: Swelling, yeast infections, things of that nature—she just had no idea.<sup>[\[304\]](#cite_note-FOOTNOTEKernes2014c-310)</sup> After hours of sex with no breaks, attempting to endure the friction of the condom in your vagina or anus is...impossible. [^cite_note-395]: &#x20;In the bible, nakedness is a source of shame. The [Book of Genesis](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Book_of_Genesis) 2\:25 says of Adam and Eve, "And they were both naked, the man and his wife, and were not ashamed." That changed when they rebelled against God's commandment and ate of the tree of knowledge. From then on, they felt ashamed in each other's presence: "And the eyes of them both were opened, and they knew that they were naked; and they sewed fig leaves together, and made themselves aprons." This biblical interpretation of nakedness as shameful still deeply informs the social norms and conventions that determine how we deal with human physicality and sexuality. Although our notions of whether, how, where and in the presence of whom a person may be undressed have changed over the centuries, the shame we feel when we transgress the norms has remained.<sup>[\[386\]](#cite_note-FOOTNOTEKämmerer2019-393)</sup>
